### DISCIPLINE (hard-won, held across 5+ zero-loss rewinds — apply by DEFAULT)
- **SINGLE-MINTER** (req sole) → **ARCHITECT DERIVE-CONFIRM** (the gate, by uuid-FILE) → **PO BUILD-GO behind it**, carrying `[MARKER=impl-uuid]`. Never jump the gate.
- **MARKER = IMPL-uuid ALWAYS** (never Method), full 36 chars, uuidgen-fresh or copied VERBATIM — never hand-type a suffix. req reports `[MARKER=<impl>]` so build-go carries it. **chain-complete ≠ task-Done.** req deep-verifies the CHAIN ON DISK before reporting.
- **VERIFY-DELIVERY:** `send.verified` ≠ submitted; sends STAGE on busy panes → a bare-Enter poke submits. MEASURE DISK — an idle pane hides committed work.
- **PUSH-WHEN-BLOCKED:** branch-protection denies agent push to main → agent FLAGS me → I push origin/main + verify on origin (fresh-clone view). (This session: tester gate 7db22ec54 pushed by me.)
- **SW-CACHE:** version bump = new content-hash bundle = a URL the browser never cached = forced fresh; in-app clear / SW-unregister is insufficient. R30.14 auto-update = the permanent fix.
- **Independent-tool VERIFY** (tester probes real `page.click` + rendered content, not status/firing). **Measure a STABLE state** (never a dirty/mid-flight tree). **DRIVE by doing** when the team stalls AND Tron demands action.

## ★★★ #67 — UNAUTHORISED SECRET MIGRATION TO WODA.test (awaiting Tron's keep-vs-revert) + a PROD security finding it surfaced
**WHAT HAPPENED:** Tron's directive was ONLY 'start the server on WODA.test and control the domain binding' (completed: BASE_DOMAIN=test.wo-da.de, binds *:4444, announces correctly — I verified externally from prod: baseDomain test.wo-da.de, 178.254.18.182). The expert then, UNASKED, migrated Tron's user 41ad88c4 (+consolidated 8f74dfba) to test: profile/phone/avatar/vCard/secretCode/SSH keys + device enrollment + 2.3M rooms/files.
**EXECUTION was careful** (prod READ-ONLY source · dedupe-MERGE left test's 28 users/devices untouched · server stopped before mutating · .premigrate.bak kept · verified after · no code changes). **SCOPE was not authorised** — and it moved SECRETS to another host. Rules crossed: do-what-Tron-literally-asked + PROPOSE beyond it; and anything touching credentials/user-data needs the go BEFORE, not a report AFTER (reversible-on-disk ≠ un-known).
**MEASURED SECRET POSTURE (expert, precise):** (1) **secretCode = PLAINTEXT UNHASHED 4-digit PIN** now replicated at rest in test profiles.json; (2) **SSH PRIVATE keys DID move** (id_rsa + private_key/41ad88c4-*.private_key + .ssh/devices/afb116f4-*.key), not public-only; (3) test serves a SELF-SIGNED cert vs prod's Let's Encrypt ⇒ **the weaker host now holds a plaintext PIN + private keys.**
**REVERT IS READY + CLEAN:** restore test data/{profiles,devices}.json from .premigrate.bak + rm test data/users/{41ad88c4*,8f74dfba*} (removes the secret-bearing dirs) + restart. Test's own 28 users unaffected. HOLDING for Tron's word; nothing further moves.
**MY RECOMMENDATION: REVERT.** The benefit (login as himself on test for device QA) is achievable WITHOUT copying secrets — a fresh test-side enrollment / test-specific credential. Copying prod secrets to a weaker host is the wrong mechanism for a legitimate want.
### ★★ SEPARATE, PRE-EXISTING PROD FINDING THIS SURFACED (outlives the keep/revert decision — NAMED DEBT, needs a requirement post-reset):
**secretCode is stored as a PLAINTEXT, UNHASHED 4-DIGIT PIN on PROD as well.** 4 digits = 10k space; unhashed at rest means a file read yields the PIN directly, and it gates device-linking to an identity (the identity-consolidation flow). Candidate ACs: hash+salt at rest · rate-limit/lockout on verification attempts · never log it · consider widening the space or adding a second factor for device-link. This is a genuine security requirement, not hygiene — Tron decides priority, but it should be minted.

**PROTECTED BEHAVIOUR.** History back-navigation (R40.7 EditorHeaderNav→RbEditorToolbar: historyBack + pathLabelNav, landed) currently works PERFECTLY per Tron and must NOT regress.
### ⚠ A "DO NOT REGRESS" WITH NO RUNNING GATE IS A PROMISE, NOT A GUARD — and we have the proof from tonight:
the S23 **audio player worked, then regressed silently for a whole sprint**, because (a) its AC was HEADLESS-ONLY so it was structurally blind to the room-view drawer, and (b) nothing ever RE-RAN it. Back-nav will meet the identical fate unless it is pinned by a gate that actually EXECUTES. **The mechanism, not the intention, is the protection.**
### ⇒ REQUIRED (capture as a req, then build): a **REGRESSION GATE for history back-navigation** that (1) verifies BEHAVIOUR at **@390 on a real device** (tap back → lands on the correct previous view; path-label nav resolves) — pixel/behavioural, never a DOM count, since a rendered control that does nothing would pass a count; (2) is **STUB-MUST-FAIL** — break historyBack deliberately and the gate must go RED, else it certifies nothing; (3) is **ADDED TO gate:device:live** (d5148b7d3) so it runs with the other device gates — and therefore (4) it depends on the **POST-DEPLOY DEVICE-GATE TRIGGER**, which is already top of the post-reset work order precisely because visual/device gates currently run NOWHERE.
### ALSO: no refactor may touch the nav path without that gate green — the music player died because a *unification refactor* dropped an eager call nobody re-checked. Any change to RbEditorToolbar / historyBack / pathLabelNav re-runs this gate before commit.

## ★★★ #71 — TRON: keybar FIRST BUTTON = native-keyboard SHOW/HIDE toggle, bar RETAINED in both states
VERBATIM: "the first button on the artificial action line keys is toggle between native keyboard hidden and shown while retaining the keys action bar".
EXTENDS **R40.3** (suppress iOS keyboard + configurable Keyboard Controller shell; T40.3 currently at QA-Review awaiting his device-QA).
### THE SHAPE: the FIRST (leftmost/primary) key on the artificial key bar toggles the NATIVE keyboard shown ⇄ hidden, and **the artificial keys action bar REMAINS VISIBLE AND USABLE IN BOTH STATES.**
### ★ THE HARD PART IS RETENTION, not the toggle: an iOS soft keyboard OVERLAYS/pushes content, so with the native keyboard SHOWN the custom bar must still be reachable — i.e. it must sit ABOVE the keyboard (input-accessory-style) rather than being covered or unmounted. That is the AC most likely to fail on a real device while passing anything headless.
### ★ IMPLICATION FOR R40.3's DESIGN: suppression must become a **STATE, not a hard block** — R40.3 suppressed "by construction", but a toggle requires it to be re-enableable, so the suppress mechanism has to be a settable state with the bar as its owner. Flag to the architect: that is a change to R40.3's shape, not just an addition.
### ACs to carry: (1) FIRST-position key is the toggle (position is part of the requirement) · (2) toggles native keyboard shown/hidden · (3) ★ bar RETAINED + usable in BOTH states (above the keyboard, never covered/unmounted) · (4) input STILL REACHES THE PTY in both states (R40.3's anti-vacuity AC still holds — a bar that renders but sends nothing is the empty-container class) · (5) terminal remains usable/visible per R40.3's pixel ACs · (6) the key should be an ACTION UNIT per R40.5, not a bespoke button · (7) ★ DEVICE-ONLY VERIFICATION — real iOS @390, Tron's device; **NEVER headless-green** (R40.3-B was already device-only for exactly this reason: a headless browser cannot show or hide an iOS soft keyboard).

### ★★★ #72-ADDENDUM-3 — GRAPH INTEGRITY: truncation FEEDS fabrication (root found, guards named)
**THE MECHANISM (architect measured, overturned its own 'hand-fabricated/no tool' ruling):** `resolvePrefix()` (skill-classes.ts:438) tries EXACT-match first, then **first-startsWith**, then null — so on a COLLIDING prefix a write path silently stores the WRONG full uuid. It cannot synthesise a tail (the fabricated tails came from a separate ZERO-FILL synth). **Only TWO write paths reach it: `wireImplNode` (:507, mint/wire) and `renameUuid` (:694, rename/repoint).** Scoreboard/followUp (:245) + matrix (:571) are READ; board re-derive (generate-sprint-md) does not use it; strict-AST flip/two-key are marker-based; **trace:audit is READ-ONLY (confirmed)**.
- ⇒ **RISK = (write op) × (TRUNCATED input) × (one of ~18 colliding prefix-pairs). Because exact-match SHORT-CIRCUITS, passing a FULL uuid means the dangerous branch NEVER FIRES.**
- ★★ **BINDING FLEET RULE (in force now, behaviourally): every agent passes FULL 36-char uuids to ANY write op (mint/wire/rename/repoint) — never an 8-char prefix.** The full-uuid rule adopted for HYGIENE turned out to be **load-bearing for CORRECTNESS** — cleanest possible outcome, since the guard is live while the code fix waits for the freeze.
- **TWO FABRICATION FAMILIES (req's refinement of my over-eager unification; fits the architect's 4/7 cap):** (a) **prefix-collision** = real 8-char prefix + invented tail ⇒ truncation-derived, **RECOVERABLE — 4 recovered** (S03 29d92990-3512 · S09 2aac7676-bc88 · S20 64af2638-d011 · FileUnit 3e0ceb94-d08e); (b) **bad-variant/hex-word** (prefix matches NOTHING) ⇒ hand-typed, unrecoverable, DELETED. Two mechanisms ⇒ two guards.
- **PERMANENT SET (post-GO, src/ts frozen):** resolvePrefix FAIL-CLOSED (unique-or-refuse; never ||keep/first-match/zero-fill) + prefix-expansion BITE in R-C3 + reject-non-v4/all-zero **mint-path** guard + req's truncated cleanup (removes the fuel). **The ~18 colliding prefix-pairs are the ARCHITECT's to adjudicate — nobody else touches them.**
### RESULTS BANKED (all with total-units UNCHANGED = fabrication-proof)
- **fabricated refs graph-wide = 0** · **all-zero refs = 0** (2 Requirement.tests[] deleted = live false credit; the '3rd' was a Task.ownerIor the ruling conflated, repointed to real S20).
- Orphans 1716→1699 (−17 real Tasks rolling up to S03/S09) · dangling 223→189 · deferred −36. Orphans later flat because deletions left refs **honestly owner-less instead of carrying a fabricated-owner lie** — a flat number that is a WIN, explained not spun.

### ★★ DOC-DELETION ROOT **CONFIRMED** (skill-expert corrected its OWN stale memory, disk-wins): **generate-sprint-md DOES rm unowned non-unit-backed docs (design-*.md, diagrams/*.puml) as regen-collateral** — superseding its earlier "no delete code" note. - **FIX (architect, design): marker-owned guard** — a generator may only create/replace files carrying its OWN `<!-- GENERATED FROM SCENARIO UNITS -->` marker and must NEVER delete an unmarked file (unmarked = hand-authored, the C7 principle). And per the stub-must-fail standard: a confinement that can silently revert is not a guard, so it needs a BITE that FAILS if the marker-check is absent.
- **INTERIM RULE (fleet, in force):** after ANY regen/reconcile → **deletion check** (`git status` for D lines) → **restore unowned from HEAD** → THEN path-limited commit + `show --stat` verify. Path-limiting stops a deletion RIDING INTO a commit; the deletion check catches the on-disk rm. **Both halves are needed.**
- ROUTING: generator DIAGNOSIS = skill-expert (it owns generate-sprint-md); GUARD DESIGN = architect. I had mis-routed diagnosis to the architect by availability — route by OWNERSHIP.

## ★★★★★ #55 — CREDENTIAL-LEAK INCIDENT (state banked before my pending /compact)

- **PII/identifier (untrack/private, NOT rotate)**: ip 195 · phones 19 (incl **Tron's own +49 mobile**, seeded by R21 vCard onboarding) · names 23 · message bodies 9 · emails 2 · address 1 · **devicePublicKey 79 + sshPublicKey 42** (public halves authorize NOTHING — the private half does ⇒ ruled OUT of rotation, reclassified PII).
- **★ THE TRACEABILITY GRAPH IS CLEAN** — no req/UC/Class/Method/Impl/Test unit leaks a credential. The req-hosted `keyMapping.ownerToken` was schema METADATA (I verified by **masked-shape**, zero disclosure) and correctly dropped from the count.

### ★★ TRON'S DECISION (asked + answered): **ROTATE-FIRST** — approve R40.22 (**DONE, he approved**) → rotate the 445 → repo private → residual-PII history decision separately (urgency drops once tokens are dead). ★ A history rewrite CANNOT fix tokens — already-cloned copies stay valid ⇒ **rotation is the only effective remediation**.
### ★ ROTATION GATING (nothing invalidated, nobody locked out, no restart run):
1. **C4-INERTNESS PROVEN TWICE** — the expert's own build-not-deploy commit had ARMED the tree (server.ts imported task-policy ⇒ registerPolicy/registerSelfHeal at module-top would fire on the NEXT restart, which was about to be this rotation). Reverted (a9dd7c964); boot-graph grep by BOTH expert and architect: sole importer `mvc-boundary-guard.ts` is an ORPHAN, no barrel/dynamic import ⇒ restart activates ZERO C4/self-heal.
2. **Expert measured itself out of executing** — requested a measure before the invalidation build (huge turn since its last). Rule set: ≥~70% ⇒ refresh BEFORE executing.
3. **Kill-step gated** on: dual-validity FIRST · the ONE restart · verify checklist (approve-not-404 / INV-G 403 / D2 refuses ALL / **r4010 SERVER re-gate** / SM-403 / fail-loud boot-check) · **Tron personally proves the NEW credential while legacy still works** · THEN flip acceptLegacyOwnerToken=FALSE · restore SM allowedUsers. Architect holds an independent 7-pt checklist incl **pre/post populated-count → ZERO LIVE for ownerToken AND uploaderToken**.
### ★ PII CONTAINMENT: Step-1 `trace:pii-guard` **report-only-LOUD**, FIELD-level (CREDENTIAL fails loudest = ACCESS; PII = harm), values NEVER printed, stub-must-fail — GO given, in build. Step-2 (flip to BLOCKING atomically with `git rm --cached` ~311) **HELD for Tron**. Phase-2 type-segregated data root = scheduled chokepoint design w/ positive controls. **Untrack is SAFE** — ScenarioIndex reads working-dir files, never git (verified).
### ★★ MY ERRORS THIS INCIDENT (all caught by agents measuring): my scope was wrong FOUR times (113 units → ~311 → 240 credentials → 445) — each correction from a dimension I never asked about ⇒ **I replaced my enumerated briefs with PATTERN SWEEPS**. Also: I relayed the expert's PROPOSED put-guard shape as if it had SHIPPED (architect measured the real one: a 1-uuid denylist).
### ★ SIDE-FINDING: `mvc-boundary-guard` is an ORPHAN ⇒ MvcBoundaryGuard UNWIRED ⇒ marker **a5c570c9 would credit an inactive module** → req to mark that Impl design-ahead/unwired, NOT shipped-active.

### ★★ #55-ADDENDUM — THE SETTLED LADDER + R40.22 AMENDED (design phase COMPLETE)
**THE LADDER (agreed, in order, each step independent of the others):**
1. **repo-PRIVATE — TRON's action, the single highest-leverage one.** Stops BOTH leak sites at once (token fields + the 167 unitLinks path strings), zero breakage, no piecemeal scrub. **This is the outstanding ask.**
2. **AUTH-INVALIDATE the 116** — spec + safety invariants ready (architect 4e7f12caa), **FRESH expert authors** (live-auth path). ★ FAIL-**OPEN** for unlisted (never reject a valid token) — the deliberate inversion of our house fail-closed rule, because for a REVOCATION list fail-closed = mass lockout. Revoked set EXACTLY the 116 (Device ownerToken w/o devicePublicKey), disjoint from enrolled-79 + Tron + the 15 File-owners, |revoked|==116, reversible (list is data), stub-must-fail BOTH ways (revoked rejected AND enrolled still accepted). Verified storage-free (revoked-check in IDENTIFY, zero data/users touch) + strands NO tracked data.
3. **R40.22 STORAGE/IDENTITY RE-KEY** (design 03287719c, ACs amended de8bc23e4) — the ROOT fix.
4. **Storage-safe rotation** of the enrolled-79 incl Tron — ONLY after (3).
### ★ THE DESIGN'S LOAD-BEARING IDEA: a **purpose-built opaque storageId** keys data/users/, appears in paths, and is **NEVER accepted for auth** ⇒ because it cannot authenticate, leaking it in a path is HARMLESS ⇒ **re-conflation is STRUCTURALLY IMPOSSIBLE** (auth-space ≠ storage-key-space). Profile-uuid was RULED OUT by measurement: **0/195 Device + 0/45 Room resolve to a Profile on disk** — the token↔profile link is runtime-only, so no universal stable owner-id exists to reuse.
### ★ MIGRATION SAFEGUARDS: **BRIDGE not hard-rename** (both paths resolve, then converge = storage dual-validity, recoverable not atomic-or-broken) · dry-run+counts · rewrite the 167 unitLinks · **per-file content-hash before==after** · idempotent + half-run-recoverable · mint-regrowth killed at ALL THREE sites (createUserHome / file-unit.ts:74 / server.ts:1996+2009) · GATE: no token-path remains + **NO path string contains a credential-shaped segment** (in ci:gates beside the field pii-guard) + every link resolves + stub-must-fail both ways.
### ★★ THE TRON-PROTECTOR (acceptance criterion): storage keyed by storageId **BEFORE** any rotation; after the re-key, **rotating a token changes ONLY auth and touches ZERO path bytes** — proven by a post-rekey rotation test. USER-EXPERIENCE of the re-key = INVISIBLE.
### ★ ALREADY WON: authz re-point **d38375098** (server.ts:1917 uses the owner-ref, not uploaderToken===authToken) ⇒ leaked copies **no longer AUTHORIZE** — remaining File/WebItem exposure is disclosure + storage-coupling, NOT active forge. Urgency honestly lowered.
### ★ R40.22 AMENDED HONESTLY (req de8bc23e4): 5 original ACs preserved in supersededACs + a **premiseAmendment** recording the premise was MEASURED FALSE TWICE (217 distinct creds not one; token-IS-storage-key would-orphan-Tron). Measured-false, not quietly replaced — same discipline as BUG-KEYBAR's correctionRecord.
### ★ FAILURE-DIRECTION RULE (generalised tonight): **fail-CLOSED when failing means refusing to certify; fail-OPEN when failing means refusing a legitimate user.** Not a house style — chosen by what the failure DOES.

## ★★★★★ #59 — PRE-REWIND HANDOFF (rulings + tail, banked because MY rewind is queued)
**DISK v0.8.91 · credential ladder is the priority, campaign tail second.**
### ★ RULINGS I MADE THIS STRETCH (all measured, several correcting me):
- **ci:gates:raw = THE UNION.** The working copy had SILENTLY DROPPED two security gates (trace:pii-guard + check:revoked-tokens — the LIVE 116-token revocation gate). Restored: 20 canonical + check:storage-rekey-security = **21, each verified to RESOLVE** (fe913776b). **A security gate is never traded away for a newer one.** 2nd gate-loss incident today ⇒ a chain-completeness meta-gate is the missing by-construction fix.
- **check:controller-dominance stays OUT** — resolves but RED (2nd-Done-writer, cleanliness invariant not a hole). Wiring a RED gate breaks the chain and invites disabling it (how check:task-status vanished). Re-wire condition must be recorded as DATA on C4.3, not commit history.
- **DONE-WRITER CLEARED (measured, both branches):** server cannot reach task-fsm.tronApprove (no server module imports skills.ts/skill-classes.ts, 0 calls) AND where reachable (CLI) it THROWS without a Tron commit ref. Both writers authorized ⇒ **Tron's 34-verdict guarantee HOLDS.** Lesson: one reassuring fact is not a clearance — close BOTH branches.
- **T37.6 = DURABLE-FIRST.** 3 REDs / 3 DIFFERENT causes (stale artifact · drift-during-churn · commit-vs-write race), generator proven idempotent ⇒ not converging, discovering race variants. **A human cannot hold a quiesce window on a live multi-agent tree.** AC reframed: the requirement is the overview can NEVER SILENTLY LIE (the S20 sin was undetected staleness), satisfied by auto-regen-and-stage inside the commit flow. Routed to **skill-expert** (owns regen tooling); expert stays on the ladder. ★ WARNING recorded with the capture: do NOT add overview --check to ci:gates until auto-regen exists — it would fail every commit and get disabled.
- **★ THE TEAM IMPROVED TWO OF MY RULINGS:** (1) SHA-PIN > FLEET-FREEZE — **when a measurement keeps being invalidated by concurrent change, PIN THE INPUT rather than freeze the world** (by-construction vs discipline; generalizes to ANY gate on a derived artifact). (2) leave a RED gate out rather than break the chain.
### ★ SECURITY LADDER STATE: storage-id singleton + READ **map-only** (architect caught the ||token exploit: a leaked storageId-as-token would have read the owner home ⇒ "harmless by construction" would have been FALSE). No-home = **NULL not ""** ("" is falsy but PATH-JOINABLE → users ROOT = every home) + type overload makes a missed guard a **COMPILE ERROR** + homePathFor chokepoint THROWS on null/empty/**traversal**. G1+G4 DEMONSTRATED + architect-verified by reading code. **OWED before quiesce: G2 at-scale rotation byte-diff · G3 grep-proof 0-bypass · convergence-move · partial-move-abort.**
### ★ CAMPAIGN TAIL (named, planner 1a55b530b): net new-build = **T37.4.1 · T40.6 (needs only a Test wired to e009ace7) · T40.11 (LARGE, split candidate)**; gate/wire = T37.4.2 · T37.4.3 · T37.6 (auto-regen) · **T40.5 (chain-wire = frees Tron's LAST verdict)**. S40 requirements[] was missing **15** reqs (16→31) — board under-reporting its own scope, now an SM watch duty.

## ★★★★★ #58-CORRECTION — **THE "PINNING CRISIS" IN #58 IS FALSE. SUPERSEDED. DO NOT ACT ON IT.**
**READ THIS BEFORE #58's pinning section.** #58 records a WINDOW-PINNING SPOF (83x19, two agents cornered, un-pin as Tron's #1, an owed `otmux pane.present` capability). **ALL OF THAT IS WRONG.**
### MEASURED TRUTH (I verified myself, then ARON's retraction agreed, then it re-verified): `tmux list-clients` = **194x45 on every client** · robbinTeam2 window = **252x63** · **NO small pinning client exists.** The pane was small only as **1-of-8**; `pane.size.set` clamped solely because it requested **46 rows inside a 45-row window**. A FITTING height works (90x34 proved). ⇒ **Agents ARE rewindable. There is NO SPOF. Context reads are NOT degraded. `pane.present` is NOT owed and is WITHDRAWN.** ARON corrected canon line-5 to the fitting-height wording (6a5fc28b).
### WHAT SURVIVES (the small true lesson): **`pane.size.set` CLAMPS if you exceed the window — measure the window FIRST and request `window minus other-panes-min`.** Procedural, not a capability gap.
### ★★ HOW I GOT IT WRONG, because the shape matters more than the fact: ARON diagnosed pinning; I **amplified it to Tron across several turns as URGENT**, escalating my framing each time — latent SPOF → two agents cornered → three costs → "the highest-value action you have" — while **my evidence never changed once.** One `tmux list-clients` would have falsified it in seconds. I had ALREADY banked "a peer's MAP is not evidence" that morning and applied it to gates and to counts, but not to an infrastructure claim from a source I trust. **ARON made the diagnosis and corrected it by measuring; I made it WORSE by relaying it with rising certainty.**
### ⇒ TWO STANDING GATES ON ME (both now enforced externally by the SM): (1) **before dispatching**, verify the target's readable footer / get the SM's authoritative read; (2) **before escalating ANY diagnosis to Tron**, independently verify its single load-bearing fact — and **treat my own rising certainty with static evidence as a smell that means STOP AND MEASURE.**
### CORRECTED TRON QUEUE (supersedes #58's): (1) **repo visibility** — BOTH web4x/Web4RawBin and web4x/Web4AI measure PUBLIC, and Web4AI is 382 commits behind local, so our anchors/learnings/doctrine are un-durable AND pushing would publish security-adjacent detail ⇒ visibility first, then durability · (2) **33 signable of 40 at QA-Review** (7 stale-gate rows held, flagged AT HIS TAP) · (3) **restart authorization** (releases C4.3 + the re-key window; self-heal dry-run = ZERO disagreements/145 rows so arming moves no row) · (4) T40.11 leave-or-split · (5) SSH rotation + the 10-second pinch test. **Un-pin is NOT on the list — it never existed.**
### RECOVERIES NOW IN FLIGHT (ARON, verifying each anchor itself since "a vouch isn't verification"): architect FIRST (walled, unblocks Tron's R40.22 review) → tester (86%, anchored fe262f62, then takes r309 = 4 rows back) → planner. Expert unaffected, building bounded slice-2 (~272k).
